I've been asked to make a zombie Pac-Man game. Let me think about it:

1. Core gameplay: Pac-Man eats dots on a maze, ghosts (zombies) chase you. Zombie theme—so instead of classic ghosts, use zombies with distinctive personalities.

2. Design considerations:
- Single HTML file, inline CSS/JS
- Canvas-based game that fills the window and resizes to fit
- Start screen displaying name and controls
- Restart functionality
- Web Audio API sound that starts after first input
- No alert/confirm/prompt
- localStorage with try/catch
- Game name in <title>

3. Zombie Pac-Man theme ideas:
- Instead of yellow dots, "brains" or "seeds"? Or glowing green orbs (toxic waste)? Let's make them glowing toxic-green pellets—"brains" might be too much. Glowing green orbs fit the zombie aesthetic.
- The player is a classic Pac-Man-style character but in zombie apocalypse colors—neon yellow/green, or maybe a "survivor." Actually let's keep it Pac-Man-like: a round chomping mouth character, neon yellow-green.
- Zombies instead of ghosts: 4 zombies with different personalities:
  - "Bram" (chaser) - red/rotting pink
  - "Shambler" (wandering) - blue-ish
  - "Stalker" (ambusher) - orange
  - "Wraith"/"Hollow" (evasive) - purple/cyan
- Instead of classic ghost eyes, zombies have glowing eyes and a stitched mouth. Drawn on Canvas: rounded body, arms outstretched forward (classic zombie pose), greenish skin with rotting spots, glowing eyes.
- Power pellets: "antidote vials" or "rad juice"—when eaten, zombies become "weakened/undead," can be eaten and give score. When eaten, a skull remains and then it disappears.
- Frenzy mode: when weakened, zombies turn pale/white with dark eyes and shake.

4. Visual design:
- Dark, foggy background, moonlight, maybe drifting mist particles
- Maze walls in sickly green/purple tones—neon toxic-green glow on the lines (classic arcade style but zombie palette)
- Scanlines/CRT effect? Could add a subtle vignette.
- HUD: score, high score, lives (small player icons), level ("night")
- Animated title start screen: "ZOMBIE PAC-MAN" with dripping text effect? Canvas or CSS. Maybe DOM-based start screen overlay is better for layout.
- Particle effects: pellets eaten (sparkle), zombie eaten (skull burst), player death (chomp animation)
- Screen shake when a zombie is nearby? Subtle.
- Background: dark gradient, drifting fog, maybe distant silhouettes of dead trees or cityscape.

- Grid-based maze. Classic Pac-Man maze is 28x31. Make something similar but with original design. Let's use a grid, e.g., 21 columns x 23 rows? The classic is 28x31 tiles. A smaller maze is easier to make and looks cleaner at small sizes. Let's go with 19x21 or the classic 28x31.

Movement system: grid-based with smooth interpolation like classic:
- Each entity has grid position (gx, gy) and pixel position. Moves in 4 directions at speed tiles/sec. Can only turn when near cell center. Queue a desired direction; if desired direction is open and we're at center, turn; else reverse if moving into wall.

Standard approach:
- pos (float tile coords, center of tile), dir (dx,dy).
- Each frame move by speed*dt. Determine current cell = round(pos). If passing through the center of a cell, check desired direction: if desired is open in that direction, set dir=desired. Else keep going; when at center and next cell in dir is wall, stop (dir becomes zero) and try desired again... The classic "can't reverse except when hitting wall" rule: reversal always allowed.

Now the zombie AI (classic-style):
- Modes: CHASE, SCATTER, FRIGHT (from power pellet), SOUL (returning to pit).
- Global timer cycles scatter(7s) → chase(20s) → scatter(7s) → chase(20s) → ... per level, maybe shortening.
- Chase target:
  - Rot (red, chaser): player's tile.
  - Cyan ("Shambler"): tile 4 ahead of player (classic Blinky).
  - Orange ("Stalker"/Inky): classic Inky uses Blinky; simpler: target = mirror of player position across maze center? Let me do: 2x(player) - blinkyTile (approximation of inky). Or just aim at a tile offset diagonally from player. Let's implement actual inky-style: target = blinky + 2*(player - blinky)? Classic Inky: take Blinky position, vector to a point 2 tiles ahead of Pac-Man, double it. Simplified: target = player + (player - blinky) → mirrors player around... that's fine.
  - Purple ("Wraith"/Pinky): classic Pinky aims 4 ahead with a bug on down rows; let me do: 4 ahead of player plus an offset toward the top? Let me just do 4 ahead (no bug)—fine.
- Scatter target: each zombie's own corner tile.
- Decision at each tile center: pick open direction (excluding reverse unless no other options) that minimizes Euclidean distance to target. Ties broken by priority up, left, down, right (classic).
- Fright: random direction choice at centers, slower speed.
- Soul: target = pit center tile, fast (2x), on arrival → rising state (timer ~1s with rise animation from below? "rising" = zombie emerges from pit: draw offset downward and slide up over 0.5s while non-collidable).

Zombie speeds (tiles/sec): base 8? Classic is ~ 11 tiles/s at 60fps, level 1 for ghosts... let me set player 8 t/s, zombies: normal 7.2, frightened 4.5, soul 14, +0.3 per night up to cap. Player slightly faster than chaser → fair.

- Direction choice function:
```
function chooseDir(e, target){ 
  // options in priority order: up, left, down, right (classic)
  const opts = [[0,-1],[-1,0],[0,1],[1,0]];
  let best=null, bd=Infinity;
  for (const [dx,dy] of opts){
    if (e.dirX===-dx && e.dirY===-dy) continue; // no reverse (unless only option)
    if (!passableZ(e.tx+dx, e.ty+dy)) continue;
    const d=(target.x-(e.tx+dx))**2+(target.y-(e.ty+dy))**2;
    if (d<bd){bd=d;best=[dx,dy];}
  }
  if(!best){ // dead end: reverse
    best=[-e.dirX,-e.dirY];
  }
  return best;
}
```
tx,ty = current tile at center. Fright: pick random from passable (no reverse) options.

  - z3 (wraith): 4 ahead but if player is above row 10 and z3 below... classic's Pinky bug shifts down by 4 on upper half—let me skip the bug, just 4 ahead.
  During fright: no target (random). Soul: (10,9) [above pit]. Scatter: corner.

Now let me write the audio synth helpers:

```js
let AC=null, master=null, muted=false;
function initAudio(){ if(AC) return; try{ AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)(); master=AC.createGain(); master.gain.value=0.45; master.connect(AC.destination);}catch(e){} }
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type='square',vol=0.2,when=0){ if(!AC||muted) return; const t=AC.currentTime+when; const o=AC.createOscillator(), g=AC.createGain(); o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f0),t); if(f1&&f1!==f0) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f1),t+dur); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+dur); o.connect(g); g.connect(master); o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+0.02);}
function noise(dur,vol=0.3,when=0,fLow=400,fHigh=2400){ if(!AC||muted)return; const t=AC.currentTime+when; const len=Math.max(1,(dur*AC.sampleRate)|0); const buf=AC.createBuffer(1,len,AC.sampleRate); const d=buf.getChannelData(0); for(let i=0;i<len;i++) d[i]=Math.random()*2-1; const src=AC.createBufferSource(); src.buffer=buf; const f=AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type='bandpass'; f.frequency.value=(fLow+fHigh)/2; f.Q.value=0.8; const g=AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+dur); src.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(master); src.start(t); }
```

decideFn for player:
```js
function playerDecide(e){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x), ty=Math.floor(e.y);
  // reverse anytime
  if(e.wantX===-e.dx && e.wantY===-e.dy && passableP(tx+e.wantX,ty+e.wantY)){ e.dx=e.wantX; e.dy=e.wantY; return; }
  if((e.wantX!==e.dx||e.wantY!==e.dy) && passableP(tx+e.wantX,ty+e.wantY)){ e.dx=e.wantX; e.dy=e.wantY; return; }
  // continue? if forward blocked → stop
  if(!(e.dx||e.dy) ) { // stopped: try to start in want dir
    if(passableP(tx+e.wantX,ty+e.wantY)){ e.dx=e.wantX; e.dy=e.wantY; }
    return;
  }
  if(!passableP(tx+e.dx,ty+e.dy)){ e.dx=0; e.dy=0; }
}
```
Note: wantDir default (0,-1)? If player never pressed a key, want = initial dir. Set want initially to left.

decideFn for zombie (normal):
```js
function zDecide(e){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x), ty=Math.floor(e.y);
  if(frightActive && e.state!=='soul'){ // random
    const opts = DIRS.filter(([dx,dy]) => !(dx===-e.dx&&dy===-e.dy) && passableZ(tx+dx,ty+dy));
    if(opts.length){ const [dx,dy]=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; e.dx=dx;e.dy=dy; }
    else { e.dx=-e.dx; e.dy=-e.dy; }
    return;
  }
  const target = getTarget(e);
  // choose best
  let best=null,bd=1e9;
  for(const [dx,dy] of DIRS){ // DIRS priority: up,left,down,right
    if(dx===-e.dx&&dy===-e.dy) continue;
    if(!passableZ(tx+dx,ty+dy)) continue;
    const d=(target.x-tx-dx)**2+(target.y-ty-dy)**2;
    if(d<bd){bd=d;best=[dx,dy];}
  }
  if(best){ e.dx=best[0];e.dy=best[1]; } else { e.dx=-e.dx;e.dy=-e.dy; } // dead end reverse
  // if no dir (stopped): pick any open
  if(!e.dx&&!e.dy){ const o=DIRS.find(([dx,dy])=>passableZ(tx+dx,ty+dy)); if(o){e.dx=o[0];e.dy=o[1];} }
}
```

Maze offscreen build:
```js
let mazeCv=document.createElement('canvas'), mazeCtx=mazeCv.getContext('2d');
function buildMaze(flash){ 
  const w=COLS*tile, h=ROWS*tile;
  mazeCv.width=w*DPR; mazeCv.height=h*DPR;
  m=mazeCtx; m.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);
  m.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
  // fill wall cells
  m.fillStyle='#0a1811';
  for(y..)for(x..) if(MAP[y][x]==='#') m.fillRect(x*tile,y*tile,tile+0.5,tile+0.5); // +0.5 to avoid seams? overlapping fine with same color
  // edges path
  const segs=[]; 
  for each wall cell (x,y): 
    if(y>0 && MAP[y-1][x]!=='#') seg.push top edge: (x*t, y*t)→((x+1)*t, y*t)

    right similar.
  stroke 3 passes.
}
```

```js
function resetRound(){
  const p=player;
  p.x=10.5;p.y=14.5;p.dx=-1;p.dy=0;p.wantX=-1;p.wantY=0;p.lastA=Math.PI;
  zombies[0].x=13.5; zombies[0].y=12.5; zombies[0].dx=-1; zombies[0].dy=0; zombies[0].state='normal';
  const pits=[[9.5,11.5,-0.8],[11.5,11.5,-2.6],[10.5,11.5,-4.4]];
  for(let i=1;i<4;i++){ const [x,y,d]=pits[i-1]; zombies[i].x=x; zombies[i].y=y; zombies[i].dx=0; zombies[i].dy=-1; zombies[i].state='rising'; zombies[i].stateT=d; }
  frightT=0; comboIdx=0; mode='chase'; modeT=Math.max(7,13-night); frostT=1.4; parts.length=0; texts.length=0;
}
```

```js
 case 'paused': break;
 case 'gameover': updateParts(dt); pitMist(dt); break;
}
```

stepEntity (final):
```js
function stepEntity(e,dt,speed,openFn,decide){
  let dist=speed*dt; let guard=0;
  while(dist>1e-6 && guard++<12){
    if(e.x<-0.5) e.x+=COLS; else if(e.x>=COLS+0.5) e.x-=COLS;
    const fx=e.x-Math.floor(e.x), fy=e.y-Math.floor(e.y);
    if(Math.abs(fx-0.5)<1e-3 && Math.abs(fy-0.5)<1e-3){ decide(e); continue; }
    if(!e.dx&&!e.dy){ // stopped: try to start from decision
      decide(e); if(!e.dx&&!e.dy) break; continue; }
    let ncx=e.x, ncy=e.y, d;
    if(e.dx>0){ ncx=Math.floor(e.x-0.5)+1.5; d=ncx-e.x; }
    else if(e.dx<0){ ncx=Math.ceil(e.x-0.5)-0.5; d=e.x-ncx; }
    else if(e.dy>0){ ncy=Math.floor(e.y-0.5)+1.5; d=ncy-e.y; }
    else { ncy=Math.ceil(e.y-0.5)-0.5; d=e.y-ncy; }
    if(d<=1e-6){ e.x===ncx? ... // numerical safety: snap and continue
      e.x=ncx; e.y=ncy; continue; }
    const mv=Math.min(d,dist);
    e.x+=e.dx*mv; e.y+=e.dy*mv; dist-=mv;
    if(mv>=d-1e-4){ e.x=ncx; e.y=ncy; }
  }
}
```
Hmm wait, subtle bug: when moving only horizontally, fy stays as-is (should already be 0.5 from a previous snap or start) ✓ all spawns are at centers and snaps keep both coords exact half-integers ✓.

Another subtlety: decide(e) at center may set dir to continue same direction → loop continues → next iteration not at center (or still at center? we're AT center; after decide, the `continue` goes back to top: fx,fy are still 0.5 → decide called again → infinite until guard! Bug!! Fix: only call decide when we've just arrived, or track a flag. Better structure: after snap, call decide once and then break out of "center handling"—restructure loop:

```js
while(dist>1e-6 && guard++<12){
  wrap...

    else { /* dir set; fall through to movement */ }
    // if still no dir after decision → stopped

  }

}
```
Let me simplify with a cleaner approach:

```js
function stepEntity(e,dt,speed,openFn,decide){
  let dist=speed*dt;
  for(let i=0;i<16 && dist>1e-6;i++){
    if(e.x<-0.5) e.x+=COLS; else if(e.x>=COLS+0.5) e.x-=COLS;
    // at center? make decision (each visit once—guaranteed by structure: after decide we move away or stop, never re-visit same center without moving)
    const fx=e.x-Math.floor(e.x), fy=e.y-Math.floor(e.y);
    if(Math.abs(fx-0.5)<2e-3 && Math.abs(fy-0.5)<2e-3){
      decide(e);
      if(!e.dx&&!e.dy) return; // stopped at dead end
      // keep looping: next iteration will move (not at center anymore? still at center!) 
    }
```
Still same issue: after decide, we're still at center → loop iterates → at center again → decide again.

The fix: after decide at a center, immediately move in the new direction for the remaining dist within the same iteration:

```js
function stepEntity(e,dt,speed,openFn,decide){
  let dist=speed*dt;
  for(let i=0;i<16 && dist>1e-6;i++){
    if(e.x<-0.5) e.x+=COLS; else if(e.x>=COLS+0.5) e.x-=COLS;
    const fx=e.x-Math.floor(e.x), fy=e.y-Math.floor(e.y);
    const atC = Math.abs(fx-0.5)<2e-3 && Math.abs(fy-0.5)<2e-3;
    if(atC){ e.x=Math.round(e.x*2)/2; e.y=Math.round(e.y*2)/2; decide(e); if(!e.dx&&!e.dy) return; }
    if(!e.dx&&!e.dy) return;
    let ncx,ncy,d;
    if(e.dx>0){ ncx=Math.floor(e.x-0.5)+1.5; d=ncx-e.x; }
    else if(e.dx<0){ ncx=Math.ceil(e.x-0.5)-0.5; d=e.x-ncx; }
    else if(e.dy>0){ ncy=Math.floor(e.y-0.5)+1.5; d=ncy-e.y; }
    else { ncy=Math.ceil(e.y-0.5)-0.5; d=e.y-ncy; }
    const mv=Math.min(d,dist);
    e.x+=e.dx*mv; e.y+=e.dy*mv; dist-=mv;
    if(mv>=d-1e-4){ e.x=ncx; e.y=ncy; } // snap; next iteration decides
  }
}
```
After decide at center, we compute the next center ahead and move—no re-decision until reaching the next center ✓. Edge: d could be ~0 if we're at center (e.g., at x=i+0.5 moving right → ncx = floor(i)+1.5 = i+1.5 → d=1 ✓ fine, never 0).

But wait—when we arrive at a center with mv>=d, we snap; next iteration: atC → decide → move toward next center. If dist runs out mid-cell, the entity rests off-center; next frame continues from there ✓. When it later reaches that center: decide ✓.

One more: wrap during movement—entity moving left in tunnel: x goes 0.5→...→ -0.5+ε? Next center from 0.5 moving left: ncx=ceil(0)-0.5 = -0.5 → d=1 → move to -0.5, snap e.x=-0.5. Next iteration: wrap check: e.x<-0.5? -0.5 is not < -0.5 (equal) → no wrap; atC: fx = -0.5 - floor(-0.5)= -0.5-(-1)=0.5 ✓ center! decide: tile = floor(-0.5) = -1 → passability checks cellAt(-1, TROW)=' ' open ✓ and neighbors... target distances use tile -1—fine numerically. Then move left more: ncx=ceil(-0.5-0.5)-0.5=ceil(-1)-0.5=-1.5, d=1 → e.x→-1.5... then wrap at <-0.5: -1.5 < -0.5 → +=21 → 19.5 ✓ center of col 19 ✓. Hmm but we moved from -0.5 to -1.5 (a full tile) before wrapping—the entity was off-screen for that sub-move; visually the position wraps instantly next frame—possible tiny teleport artifact at tunnel edge? The player would appear to jump from x=-1.5+... wait no: within one stepEntity call, after snap to -0.5, we continue moving (if dist remains) to -1.5 then wrap to 19.5—all in the same frame → final drawn position is 19.5 ✓ no visible artifact as long as all happens within one frame ✓. But if dist runs out at x=-0.7 (off-screen by 0.2 tile, half a cell width past edge?)—x=-0.7 means 0.2 tiles left of center -0.5 → pixel position is slightly past the maze's left edge; drawn there (margin area) for one frame then wraps. Slight visual pop at tunnel boundary. Mitigate: wrap threshold tighter: if(e.x<-0.49)... no—let me wrap earlier: when e.x < 0 - tile? Hmm. Alternative: wrap when x <= -0.5 exactly, i.e., `if(e.x<=-0.5) e.x+=COLS` (use ≤). Then snap to -0.5 → immediately wraps to 20.5 in the same iteration start... wait order: at top of loop we wrap before atC check: e.x=-0.5 → ≤ → +=21 → 20.5 ✓ then atC(20.5) ✓ decide at tile 20 ✓ move left toward 19.5 ✓. And if dist runs out mid-move to -0.5 (say at x=-0.3): not wrapped yet (>-0.5), drawn slightly past edge (0.3-0.5=…x=-0.3 is 0.2 tiles left of center 0.5? pixel = -0.3*tile → 0.3 tiles left of maze origin—slightly outside). Next frame: continues to -0.5 then wraps. So at most ~1-2 frames with entity straddling the edge—actually that's correct visual for entering the tunnel (it exits the visible maze and re-enters on the other side) ✓ fine! The margin area is dark bg; acceptable, classic does similar.

Use ≤ for left wrap: `if(e.x<=-0.5) e.x+=COLS; else if(e.x>=COLS+0.5?` right side: from 20.5 moving right → next center = floor(20)+1.5=21.5 → snap at 21.5 → wrap `if(e.x>=COLS-0.5+1)`... 21.5 = COLS+0.5 → `if(e.x>=COLS+0.5) e.x-=COLS` → 0.5 ✓ (use ≥).

playerDecide:
```js
function playerDecide(e){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x), ty=Math.floor(e.y);
  if(open(tx+e.wantX,ty+e.wantY)){ e.dx=e.wantX; e.dy=e.wantY; return; }
  if(!open(tx+e.dx,ty+e.dy)){ e.dx=0; e.dy=0; }
}
```
At center: if want is open → follow (covers turn + reverse + straight). Else if forward blocked → stop. Note: when stopped at dead end and player presses a key, want changes; next frame: stepEntity → atC? They're at center (stopped there) → decide → want open (reverse) → move ✓.

Also mid-cell reversal pre-check in updatePlaying before stepEntity—actually playerDecide already handles reverse at centers; the mid-cell pre-check adds responsiveness ✓ keep:
```js
if((player.wantX===-player.dx&&player.wantY===-player.dy)&&(player.dx||player.dy)){ const tx=Math.floor(player.x),ty=Math.floor(player.y); if(open(tx+player.wantX,ty+player.wantY)){ player.dx=player.wantX; player.dy=player.wantY; } }
```

frightDecide:
```js
function frightDecide(e){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x), ty=Math.floor(e.y);
  const opts=DIRS.filter(([a,b])=>!((a===-e.dx&&b===-e.dy)) && open(tx+a,ty+b));
  if(opts.length){ const [a,b]=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; e.dx=a; e.dy=b; }
  else { e.dx=-e.dx|| (open(tx,ty-1)?0:0)... 
```
Careful: reversing from (0,0) gives (0,0). In fright mode zombies never stop (decide always sets a dir unless no options; if no options including reverse → we're boxed in—impossible). If opts empty and e.dx!=0 → reverse ✓. If e.dx==0 (shouldn't happen) pick any open:
```js
  else { const o=DIRS.find(([a,b])=>open(tx+a,ty+b)); if(o){e.dx=o[0];e.dy=o[1];} else {e.dx=-e.dx;e.dy=-e.dy;} }
```

drawZombie(z): as designed. Let me write concrete code:
```js
function drawZombie(z){
  const s=tile; 
  let off=0, alpha=1;
  if(z.state==='rising'){ const pr=clamp(z.stateT/RISEDUR,0,1); off=(1-pr)*s*1.05; alpha=.35+.65*pr; }
  const px=z.x*s, py=z.y*s+off;
  let r=s*0.42;
  // facing angle
  if(z.dx||z.dy) z.dirA=Math.atan2(z.dy,z.dx);
  const a=z.dirA;
  if(z.state==='soul'){ drawSoul(z,px,py,r); return; }
  const fright=frightT>0 && state!=='menu';
  let flash=false;
  if(fright && frightT<1.6) flash = Math.sin(T*24)>0;
  const col = (fright&&!flash)? '#dfe9ee' : z.color;
  const darkCol=(fright&&!flash)? '#37505e' : 'rgba(10,10,14,.55)';
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
  // shadow
  if(off<2){ ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.3)'; ellipse(px, py+r*0.85, r*.8, r*.28); }
  // tremble when frightened (not flashing)
  let tx=0,ty=0; if(fright&&!flash){ tx=rnd(-1.2,1.2); ty=rnd(-1.2,1.2); }
  ctx.translate(px+tx,py+ty);
  // arms (behind body? draw before body so they peek) — actually arms stick out sideways-forward; draw them first
  ctx.save(); ctx.rotate(a);
  ctx.strokeStyle=col; ctx.lineWidth=r*0.34; ctx.lineCap='round';
  line from (r*.15, -r*.52) to (r*.95, -r*.62)? arms angled slightly up-forward: 
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r*.2,-r*.45); ctx.lineTo(r*1.0,-r*.6); ctx.moveTo(r*.2,r*.45); ctx.lineTo(r*1.0,r*.6); ctx.stroke();
  // hand circles? lineCap round gives rounded ends ✓
  ctx.restore();
  // body path (local coords, unrotated—blob is symmetric)
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(0,-r*.08,r,Math.PI,0);
  ctx.lineTo(r, r*.5);
  const n=4;
  for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){ const bx=r-(2*r*i)/n; const by=r*.5+(i%2? r*.34 : r*.12)*z.jag[i-1]; ctx.lineTo(bx,by); }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle=col; ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,r*.08); ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(5,10,8,.5)'; ctx.stroke();
  // face: eyes offset toward facing
  const ex=Math.cos(a)*r*.12, ey=Math.sin(a)*r*.12;
  if(fright&&!flash){
    // dark slits
    ctx.fillStyle=darkCol... '#1c2f3a';
    for(sg of[-1,1]){ ctx.save(); ctx.translate(ex+sg*r*.34, -r*.18+ey); ctx.rotate(a*0.3? no—slit vertical: small rect) 
      ctx.fillRect(-r*.05,-r*.16,r*.1,r*.26); ctx.restore(); }
  } else {
    for(sg of[-1,1]){
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; circle(ex+sg*r*.34, -r*.18+ey, r*.17);
      ctx.fillStyle= darkCol; circle(ex+sg*r*.34+Math.cos(a)*r*.07, -r*.18+ey+Math.sin(a)*r*.07, r*.09);
    }
  }
  // stitched mouth
  ctx.strokeStyle=(fright&&!flash)?'#243a46':'rgba(12,14,18,.75)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,r*.07);
  const my=r*.3;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-r*.34,my); ctx.lineTo(r*.34,my); 
  for(t of[-.18,0,.18]){ ctx.moveTo(t*r,my-r*.1); ctx.lineTo(t*r,my+r*.1); }
  ctx.stroke();
  // rot spots
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(8,12,10,.3)';
  for(const [sx,sy] of z.spot){ circle(sx*r, sy*r - r*.08, r*.13); }
  ctx.restore();
}
```
Wait—slit eyes: I wrote fillRect in rotated local frame; fine.

Hmm, arm rotation: arms rotate with facing ✓ but body unrotated (blob is symmetric) ✓ good.

drawSoul:
```js
function drawSoul(z,px,py,r){
  const bob=Math.sin(T*12+z.idx)*r*.1;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=.95;
  // trail
  for(let k=3;k>=1;k--){ ctx.fillStyle=z.color; ctx.globalAlpha=.08*k? use .1/.07/.04; circle(px - Math.cos(z.dirA)*k*r*.6, py + bob - Math.sin(z.dirA)*k*r*.6, r*(.5-.09*k)); }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  for(sg of[-1,1]){ 
    glow: 'rgba(200,255,255,.25)' circle radius r*.4 at (sg*r*.32);
    core '#eaffff' circle r*.2; pupil dark? soul eyes: white orbs with dark center = creepy ✓: dark '#10202a' circle r*.09.
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
```


buildMazeLayer(flash):
```js
function buildMazeLayer(flash){
  const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=Math.max(1,COLS*tile*DPR|0); c.height=Math.max(1,ROWS*tile*DPR|0);
  const g=c.getContext('2d'); g.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);
  const t=tile;
  // wall fill
  g.fillStyle='#0a1811';
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++) if(MAP[y][x]==='#') g.fillRect(x*t-0.5,y*t-0.5,t+1,t+1);
  // edge segments
  const seg=[];
  const isW=(x,y)=> y>=0&&y<ROWS&&x>=0&&x<COLS&&MAP[y][x]==='#';
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++){ if(!isW(x,y))continue;
    if(!isW(x,y-1)) seg.push([x*t,y*t,x*t+t,y*t]);
    if(!isW(x,y+1)) seg.push([x*t,(y+1)*t,x*t+t,(y+1)*t]);
    if(!isW(x-1,y)) seg.push([x*t,y*t,x*t,(y+1)*t]);
    if(!isW(x+1,y)) seg.push([(x+1)*t,y*t,(x+1)*t,(y+1)*t]);
  }
  const path=()=>{ g.beginPath(); for(const s of seg){ g.moveTo(s[0],s[1]); g.lineTo(s[2],s[3]); } };
  g.lineCap='round'; g.lineJoin='round';
  if(flash){ 
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,240,170,.28)'; g.lineWidth=t*0.4; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,235,160,.85)'; g.lineWidth=t*0.18; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='#fffbe6'; g.lineWidth=t*0.09; g.stroke();
  } else {
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(40,255,140,.16)'; g.lineWidth=t*0.42; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(80,255,170,.5)'; g.lineWidth=t*0.2; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='#c9ffe3'; g.lineWidth=t*0.09; g.stroke();
  }
  return c;
}
```
isW with x=-1 → x>=0 false → not wall → edge drawn at col 0's left side?? For a wall cell at x=0, isW(-1,y)=false → we'd draw a left edge segment at x=0 — but the maze border: drawing an outer boundary line around the whole maze. That's actually desirable (maze has an outline). But in the tunnel row, MAP[11][0]='.' so no wall cell there → gap ✓. Wait—but for row 10 col 0 ('#'), isW(0,9) above... fine. And at x=COLS-1 right edge: isW(COLS,y)=false → outer right boundary drawn ✓. Top/bottom boundaries ✓. Good—the whole maze gets a neon frame + inner lines. 

But hmm—outer boundary at col 0 left edge: segment (0*t, y*t)→(0,t*(y+1)) — sits at canvas origin line; half of the stroke would be clipped outside the offscreen canvas (canvas starts at 0). Half-clipped glow = slightly thinner outer line. Acceptable; or pad the offscreen by t/2 margin and offset draws. Let me add a margin: make canvas (COLS*t + t) x (ROWS*t + t), draw everything with +t/2 offset... simpler: extend canvas by 8px each side: c.width=(COLS*t+16)*DPR; g.translate(8,8); and blit at (ox-8, oy-8) with size COLS*t+16. Let me do that for clean outer glow ✓.

```js
  // inner glow
  const cg=ctx.createRadialGradient(10.5*t,11.5*t,2, 10.5*t,11.5*t,t*1.5);
  cg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,60,200,.4)'); cg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(120,60,200,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle=cg; circle fill at center radius t*1.5 (fillRect over pit area).
  // breathing ring
  ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(150,90,230,${.25+.18*Math.sin(T*2.4)})`; lw 2; ellipse(10.5t,11.5t, t*(1.05+.06*sin), t*.42); stroke.
  // skull
  drawSkull(10.5*t, 11.5*t + Math.sin(T*2.4)*1.5, t*0.34);
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawSkull(x,y,s){
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x,y);
  ctx.fillStyle='#e9e6d4';
  circle(0,-s*0.15,s*0.62); // cranium (radius s*.62? let me parametrize: r=s*0.6)
  // jaw
  rrect(-s*0.34, s*0.18, s*0.68, s*0.34, s*0.1); fill;
  // eyes
  ctx.fillStyle='#120a1e';
  circle(-s*0.24,-s*0.2,s*0.17); circle(s*0.24,-s*0.2,s*0.17);
  // nose
  triangle: path (0,-s*0.02)(-s*0.09,s*0.12)(s*0.09,s*0.12) fill;
  // teeth gaps
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,10,30,.8)'; lw Math.max(1,s*.06);
  for k of [-1,1]: line (k*s*0.15, s*0.2)→(k*s*0.15, s*0.5);
  // glow behind? add subtle: before fill ctx shadow? skip; add outer soft circle 'rgba(180,140,255,.12)' radius s*1.3 first.
  ctx.restore();
}
```

Mist particles drawn in drawParts with type 'mist' → purple-ish rgba(140,90,220,alpha*.35) circle growing: r = p.size*(1+ (1-p.life/p.max)*1.5).

burst(): parts.push({type:'frag',x,y,vx:cos*sp,vy:sin*sp,rot:rnd(6.28),vr:rnd(-8,8),size:rnd(t*.07,t*.16),life:rnd(.4,.9),max:same,color}).

```js
 if(p.type==='mist'){ ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(150,95,225,${a*.3})`; circle(px,py,p.size*(2-a)); }
```

```js
function looseZombies(dt){
  for(const z of zombies){
    if(z.state==='rising'){ z.stateT+=dt; if(z.stateT>=RISEDUR){z.state='normal';} continue; }
    if(z.state==='soul'){ soulFly(z,dt); continue; }
    // wander
    const spd=4.2;
    stepEntity(z,dt,spd,isOpen,(e)=>{
      const tx=Math.floor(e.x),ty=Math.floor(e.y);
      const opts=DIRS.filter(([a,b])=>isOpen(tx+a,ty+b));
      if(opts.length){ const [a,b]=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; e.dx=a;e.dy=b; } else { e.dx=-e.dx||1; e.dy=-(e.dx? -e.dy:0); } 
```
Hmm that else is garbage—simplify: `else { e.dx=-e.dx; e.dy=-e.dy; if(!e.dx&&!e.dy){e.dx=1;} }` fine (won't happen anyway).

soulFly(z,dt):
```js
function soulFly(z,dt){
  const tx=10.5,ty=11.5; let dx=tx-z.x, dy=ty-z.y; const d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1e-6;
  z.dirA=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const s=SOULSPD*dt;
  if(d<=s+0.3){ const cx=clamp(Math.round(z.x-0.5),9,11); z.x=cx+0.5; z.y=11.5; z.state='rising'; z.stateT=-0.7; sfxRise(); }
  else { z.x+=dx/d*s; z.y+=dy/d*s; }
}
```

updateZombie (playing):
```js
function updateZombie(z,dt){
  if(z.state==='rising'){ z.stateT+=dt; if(z.stateT>=RISEDUR){ z.state='normal'; z.dx=0; z.dy=-1; } return; }
  if(z.state==='soul'){ soulFly(z,dt); return; }
  const fright=frightT>0;
  const spd= fright? FRIGHTSPD : Math.min(7.0+0.38*(night-1),7.8)*z.spd;
  stepEntity(z,dt,spd,isOpen, fright?frightDecide:(e)=>zDecide(e,z));
}
```

(syncHUD handles lives icons.)

- eatDots:
```js
function eatDots(){
  const tx=Math.floor(player.x),ty=Math.floor(player.y);
  if(Math.abs(player.x-tx-0.5)>0.3||Math.abs(player.y-ty-0.5)>0.3) return;
  const d=dots[ty][tx]; if(!d) return;
  dots[ty][tx]=0; dotsLeft--;
  if(d===1){ addScore(10); sfxWaka(); sparkBurst(player.x,player.y); }
  else { addScore(50); triggerFright(); }
  if(dotsLeft<=0 && state==='playing'){ state='clearing'; clearT=0; sfxClear(); frightT=0; }
}
```

```html
<script>
(()=>{'use strict';
/* ============================== helpers ============================== */
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>v<a?a:v>b?b:v;
const rnd=(a,b)=>a+Math.random()*(b-a);
const TAU=Math.PI*2;

/* ============================== maze ============================== */
const COLS=21,ROWS=23,TROW=11,RISEDUR=1.0;
const MAP=[
"#####################",
"#.........#.........#",
"#.##..###.#.###..##.#",
"#o##..###.#.###..##o#",
"#.##..#.#.#.#.#..##.#",
"#..................#",
"#.##..###.#.###..##.#",
"#.##..#.#.#.#.#..##.#",
"#......##...##.....#",
"#####.###...###.#####",
"#.....###...###.....#",
"..###....XXX....###..",
"#.##..#.......##.#",
"#.##..###...###..##.#",
"#..................#",
"#.##..###.#.###..##.#",
"#.##..#.#.#.#.#..##.#",
"#o##..###.#.###..##o#",
"#......##...##.....#",
"#..................#",
"#.##..#.#.#.#.#..##.#",
"#.###.#...#...#.###.#",
"#####################"
].map(r=>r.padEnd(COLS,'#').slice(0,COLS));

const isOpen=(x,y)=>{ if(y<0||y>=ROWS) return false; if(x<0||x>=COLS) return y===TROW; const c=MAP[y][x]; return c!=='#'&&c!=='X'; };
const DIRS=[[0,-1],[-1,0],[0,1],[1,0]];

/* ============================== audio ============================== */
let AC=null,master=null,muted=false;
try{ muted = localStorage.getItem('zp_mute')==='1'; }catch(e){}
function initAudio(){
  if(AC){ if(AC.state==='suspended') AC.resume(); return; }
  try{
    AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    master=AC.createGain(); master.gain.value=muted?0:0.45; master.connect(AC.destination);
  }catch(e){}
}
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,when){
  if(!AC||muted) return; type=type||'square'; vol=vol||.2; when=when||0;
  const t=AC.currentTime+when,o=AC.createOscillator(),g=AC.createGain();
  o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f0),t);
  if(f1&&f1!==f0) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1,f1),t+dur);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);
  o.connect(g); g.connect(master); o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+.03);
}
function noise(dur,vol,when,fLo,fHi){
  if(!AC||muted) return; vol=vol||.25; when=when||0;
  const t=AC.currentTime+when,len=Math.max(1,(dur*AC.sampleRate)|0);
  const buf=AC.createBuffer(1,len,AC.sampleRate),d=buf.getChannelData(0);
  for(let i=0;i<len;i++) d[i]=Math.random()*2-1;
  const src=AC.createBufferSource(); src.buffer=buf;
  const f=AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type='bandpass'; f.frequency.value=(fLo+fHi)/2; f.Q.value=.8;
  const g=AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);
  src.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(master); src.start(t);
}
let wakaFlip=false;
const sfxWaka =()=>{ wakaFlip=!wakaFlip; tone(wakaFlip?430:310,wakaFlip?310:430,.07,'square',.13); };
const sfxPower=()=>{ tone(880,160,.5,'sawtooth',.2); noise(.25,.12,0,900,3200); };
const sfxSiren=n=>{ tone(n%2?240:170,n%2?160:240,.3,'triangle',.14); };
const sfxEat  =()=>{ noise(.22,.3,0,700,3400); tone(380,60,.28,'square',.22); };
const sfxDie  =()=>{ tone(520,45,1.0,'sawtooth',.28); tone(300,30,.9,'square',.18,.1); noise(.5,.15,.15,200,900); };
const sfxClear=()=>{ [523,659,784,1047].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f*1.01,.14,'square',.18,i*.12)); };
const sfxRise =()=>{ tone(75,38,.45,'sine',.5); noise(.35,.16,0,90,500); };
const sfxExtra=()=>{ [660,880,1320,1760].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,.1,'square',.17,i*.09)); };
const sfxStart=()=>{ tone(520,780,.12,'square',.18); tone(780,1040,.14,'square',.16,.1); };
function toggleMute(){ muted=!muted; if(master) master.gain.value=muted?0:0.45;
  try{ localStorage.setItem('zp_mute',muted?'1':'0'); }catch(e){}
  $('sndLbl').textContent='M · SOUND '+(muted?'OFF':'ON'); }

/* ============================== state ============================== */
let W=0,H=0,DPR=1,tile=8,ox=0,oy=0;
let state='menu',T=0,score=0,lives=3,night=1,dotsLeft=0,dots=[];
let frightT=0,comboIdx=0,mode='chase',modeT=12,frostT=0,deathT=0,clearT=0;
let extraLifeAt=10000,newHigh=false,sirenAcc=0,sirenN=0,nextBeat=0;
let shakeT=0,shakeDur=1,shakeMag=0;
let high=0; try{ high=+localStorage.getItem('zp_high')||0; }catch(e){}
function saveHigh(){ try{ localStorage.setItem('zp_high',String(high)); }catch(e){} }
const PSPEED=8.4,SOULSPD=11,FRIGHTSPD=4.0;

const player={x:10.5,y:14.5,dx:-1,dy:0,wantX:-1,wantY:0,lastA:Math.PI};
const ZDEF=[
 {name:'ROT',     color:'#ff5c5c',corner:{x:19,y:2}, spd:1.04,jag:[1,.6,1.2,.7], spot:[[-.45,-.35],[.4,.15]]},
 {name:'SHAMBLER',color:'#52e0d8',corner:{x:1, y:2}, spd:.97, jag:[.7,1.1,.6,1.2],spot:[[.5,-.25],[-.35,.3]]},
 {name:'STALKER', color:'#ffb454',corner:{x:1, y:20},spd:1.0, jag:[1.1,.7,1,.6],  spot:[[-.5,.1],[.3,-.4]]},
 {name:'WRAITH',  color:'#b78bff',corner:{x:19,y:20},spd:.94, jag:[.8,1.2,.7,1],  spot:[[.45,-.3],[-.4,-.15]]}
];
let zombies=[];
function makeZombies(){ zombies=ZDEF.map((d,i)=>Object.assign({idx:i,x:0,y:0,dx:-1,dy:0,state:'normal',stateT:0,dirA:Math.PI},d)); }

const parts=[],texts=[],spores=[],fog=[];
let mazeN=null,mazeF=null;

function loadDots(){
  dots=MAP.map(row=>row.split('').map(c=>c==='.'?1:c==='o'?2:0));
  dotsLeft=0; for(const r of dots) for(const c of r) if(c) dotsLeft++;
}
function resetRound(){
  player.x=10.5;player.y=14.5;player.dx=-1;player.dy=0;player.wantX=-1;player.wantY=0;player.lastA=Math.PI;
  const z=zombies;
  z[0].x=13.5;z[0].y=12.5;z[0].dx=-1;z[0].dy=0;z[0].state='normal';z[0].dirA=Math.PI;
  const pits=[[9.5,-0.8],[11.5,-2.6],[10.5,-4.4]];
  for(let i=1;i<4;i++){ z[i].x=pits[i-1][0]; z[i].y=11.5; z[i].dx=0; z[i].dy=-1; z[i].state='rising'; z[i].stateT=pits[i-1][1]; }
  frightT=0;comboIdx=0;mode='chase';modeT=Math.max(7,13-night);frostT=1.4;
  parts.length=0;texts.length=0;sirenAcc=0;nextBeat=0;
}
function startGame(){
  score=0;lives=3;night=1;extraLifeAt=10000;newHigh=false;
  makeZombies(); loadDots(); resetRound();
  state='playing';
  $('startOv').classList.add('hidden'); $('goOv').classList.add('hidden'); $('pauseOv').classList.add('hidden');
  pushText('NIGHT 1',10.5,8.5,'#ffd93b',Math.round(tile*.9),2);
  sfxStart(); syncHUD(true);
}
function gameOver(){
  state='gameover';
  if(score>high){ high=score; saveHigh(); newHigh=true; }
  $('goScore').textContent=String(score).padStart(6,'0');
  $('goNight').textContent=String(night);
  $('newHi').classList.toggle('hidden',!newHigh);
  $('startHigh').textContent=String(high).padStart(6,'0');
  $('goOv').classList.remove('hidden');
  syncHUD(true);
}

/* ============================== movement core ============================== */
function stepEntity(e,dt,speed,decide){
  let dist=speed*dt;
  for(let i=0;i<16&&dist>1e-6;i++){
    if(e.x<=-0.5) e.x+=COLS; else if(e.x>=COLS+0.5) e.x-=COLS;
    const fx=e.x-Math.floor(e.x), fy=e.y-Math.floor(e.y);
    if(Math.abs(fx-0.5)<2e-3 && Math.abs(fy-0.5)<2e-3){
      e.x=Math.round(e.x*2)/2; e.y=Math.round(e.y*2)/2;
      decide(e);
      if(!e.dx&&!e.dy) return;
    }
    if(!e.dx&&!e.dy) return;
    let ncx=e.x,ncy=e.y,d;
    if(e.dx>0){ ncx=Math.floor(e.x-0.5)+1.5; d=ncx-e.x; }
    else if(e.dx<0){ ncx=Math.ceil(e.x-0.5)-0.5; d=e.x-ncx; }
    else if(e.dy>0){ ncy=Math.floor(e.y-0.5)+1.5; d=ncy-e.y; }
    else { ncy=Math.ceil(e.y-0.5)-0.5; d=e.y-ncy; }
    const mv=Math.min(d,dist);
    e.x+=e.dx*mv; e.y+=e.dy*mv; dist-=mv;
    if(mv>=d-1e-4){ e.x=ncx; e.y=ncy; }
  }
}
function playerDecide(e){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x),ty=Math.floor(e.y);
  if(isOpen(tx+e.wantX,ty+e.wantY)){ e.dx=e.wantX; e.dy=e.wantY; return; }
  if(!isOpen(tx+e.dx,ty+e.dy)){ e.dx=0; e.dy=0; }
}
function frightDecide(e){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x),ty=Math.floor(e.y);
  const opts=DIRS.filter(([a,b])=>!((a===-e.dx&&b===-e.dy))&&isOpen(tx+a,ty+b));
  if(opts.length){ const [a,b]=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; e.dx=a; e.dy=b; }
  else { const o=DIRS.find(([a,b])=>isOpen(tx+a,ty+b)); if(o){e.dx=o[0];e.dy=o[1];} else {e.dx=-e.dx;e.dy=-e.dy;} }
}
function zTarget(z){
  if(mode==='scatter') return z.corner;
  const px=Math.floor(player.x),py=Math.floor(player.y);
  switch(z.idx){
    case 0: return {x:px,y:py};
    case 1: return {x:px+player.dx*4,y:py+player.dy*4};
    case 2: { const A=zombies[0],bx=px+player.dx*2,by=py+player.dy*2; return {x:2*bx-A.x,y:2*by-A.y}; }
    default: return {x:COLS-1-px,y:ROWS-1-py};
  }
}
function zDecide(e,z){
  const tx=Math.floor(e.x),ty=Math.floor(e.y),tg=zTarget(z);
  let best=null,bd=1e9;
  for(const [a,b] of DIRS){
    if(a===-e.dx&&b===-e.dy) continue;
    if(!isOpen(tx+a,ty+b)) continue;
    const d=(tg.x-tx-a)*(tg.x-tx-a)+(tg.y-ty-b)*(tg.y-ty-b);
    if(d<bd){ bd=d; best=[a,b]; }
  }
  if(best){ e.dx=best[0]; e.dy=best[1]; }
  else { const o=DIRS.find(([a,b])=>isOpen(tx+a,ty+b)); if(o){e.dx=o[0];e.dy=o[1];} else {e.dx=-e.dx;e.dy=-e.dy;} }
}
function updateZombie(z,dt){
  if(z.state==='rising'){ z.stateT+=dt; if(z.stateT>=RISEDUR){ z.state='normal'; z.dx=0; z.dy=-1; } return; }
  if(z.state==='soul'){ soulFly(z,dt); return; }
  const fright=frightT>0;
  const spd=fright?FRIGHTSPD:Math.min(7.0+0.38*(night-1),7.8)*z.spd;
  stepEntity(z,dt,spd,fright?frightDecide:(e)=>zDecide(e,z));
}
function soulFly(z,dt){
  const dx=10.5-z.x,dy=11.5-z.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1e-6;
  z.dirA=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const s=SOULSPD*dt;
  if(d<=s+0.3){ z.x=clamp(Math.round(z.x-0.5),9,11)+0.5; z.y=11.5; z.state='rising'; z.stateT=-0.7; sfxRise(); }
  else { z.x+=dx/d*s; z.y+=dy/d*s; }
}
function looseZombies(dt){
  for(const z of zombies){
    if(z.state==='rising'){ z.stateT+=dt; if(z.stateT>=RISEDUR) z.state='normal'; continue; }
    if(z.state==='soul'){ soulFly(z,dt); continue; }
    stepEntity(z,dt,4.2,(e)=>{
      const tx=Math.floor(e.x),ty=Math.floor(e.y);
      const opts=DIRS.filter(([a,b])=>isOpen(tx+a,ty+b));
      if(opts.length){ const [a,b]=opts[(Math.random()*opts.length)|0]; e.dx=a; e.dy=b; }
    });
  }
}

/* ============================== gameplay events ============================== */
function addScore(n){
  score+=n;
  if(score>=extraLifeAt){ extraLifeAt+=20000; lives++; sfxExtra(); pushText('EXTRA LIFE!',player.x,player.y-1.2,'#ffd93b',Math.round(tile*.6)); }
  syncHUD();
}
function triggerFright(){ frightT=Math.max(2.5,6-(night-1)*0.55); comboIdx=0; sirenAcc=0; sfxPower(); }
function eatDots(){
  const tx=Math.floor(player.x),ty=Math.floor(player.y);
  if(Math.abs(player.x-tx-0.5)>0.3||Math.abs(player.y-ty-0.5)>0.3) return;
  const d=dots[ty][tx]; if(!d) return;
  dots[ty][tx]=0; dotsLeft--;
  if(d===1){ addScore(10); sfxWaka(); sparkBurst(player.x,player.y); }
  else { addScore(50); triggerFright(); }
  if(dotsLeft<=0&&state==='playing'){ state='clearing'; clearT=0; frightT=0; sfxClear(); }
}
function eatZombie(z){
  const pts=[200,400,800,1600][Math.min(comboIdx,3)]; comboIdx++;
  addScore(pts);
  pushText(String(pts),z.x,z.y-0.5,'#eafff0',Math.round(tile*.55));
  burst(z.x,z.y,z.color,18);
  z.state='soul'; sfxEat(); shake(.25,5);
}
function startDeath(){
  state='dying'; deathT=0; lives--;
  burst(player.x,player.y,'#ffd93b',26);
  shake(.5,8); sfxDie(); syncHUD(true);
}
function checkCollide(){
  for(const z of zombies){
    if(z.state!=='normal') continue;
    if(Math.hypot(z.x-player.x,z.y-player.y)<0.62){
      if(frightT>0) eatZombie(z); else { startDeath(); return; }
    }
  }
}

/* ============================== particles / text ============================== */
function pushText(txt,x,y,color,size,life){ texts.push({txt,x,y,color,size:size||Math.round(tile*.55),life:life||1.3,t:0}); }
function burst(x,y,color,n){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ const a=rnd(0,TAU),s=rnd(2,6);
    parts.push({type:'frag',x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s,rot:rnd(0,TAU),vr:rnd(-8,8),size:rnd(tile*.07,tile*.16),life:rnd(.4,.9),max:1,color});
    const L=parts[parts.length-1].life; parts[parts.length-1].max=L; }
}
function sparkBurst(x,y){
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){ const a=rnd(0,TAU),s=rnd(.8,2.6);
    parts.push({type:'spark',x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*s,vy:Math.sin(a)*s,size:rnd(tile*.03,tile*.06),life:.3,max:.3,color:'#a6ffcf'}); }
}
let mistAcc=0;
function pitMist(dt){
  mistAcc+=dt;
  if(mistAcc>0.28){ mistAcc=0;
    parts.push({type:'mist',x:rnd(9.3,11.7),y:11.5,vx:0,vy:-rnd(.25,.5),size:tile*rnd(.12,.24),life:1.6,max:1.6,color:''}); }
}
function updateParts(dt){
  for(let i=parts.length-1;i>=0;i--){ const p=parts[i];
    p.x+=p.vx*dt; p.y+=p.vy*dt; if(p.type==='frag'){ p.rot+=p.vr*dt; p.vx*=.985; p.vy*=.985; }
    p.life-=dt; if(p.life<=0) parts.splice(i,1); }
  if(parts.length>420) parts.splice(0,parts.length-420);
  for(let i=texts.length-1;i>=0;i--){ texts[i].t+=dt; if(texts[i].t>texts[i].life) texts.splice(i,1); }
}

/* ============================== ambient background ============================== */
function initAmbient(){
  if(!spores.length){ for(let i=0;i<42;i++) spores.push({x:rnd(0,W),y:rnd(0,H),spd:rnd(7,20),tw:rnd(.5,1.6),ph:rnd(0,TAU),s:rnd(.6,1.9)}); }
  if(!fog.length){ for(let i=0;i<4;i++) fog.push({x:rnd(0,W),y:rnd(H*.2,H*.85),r:rnd(Math.min(W,H)*.3,Math.min(W,H)*.55),vx:(i%2?1:-1)*rnd(5,13),a:rnd(.04,.07)}); }
}
function updateAmbient(dt){
  for(const s of spores){ s.y-=s.spd*dt; if(s.y<-6){ s.y=H+6; s.x=rnd(0,W); } }
  for(const f of fog){ f.x+=f.vx*dt; if(f.x>W+f.r) f.x=-f.r; else if(f.x<-f.r) f.x=W+f.r; }
}

/* ============================== maze layer pre-render ============================== */
function buildMazeLayer(flash){
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round((COLS*tile+16)*DPR)); c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round((ROWS*tile+16)*DPR));
  const g=c.getContext('2d'); g.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0); g.translate(8,8);
  const t=tile;
  g.fillStyle='#0a1811';
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++) if(MAP[y][x]==='#') g.fillRect(x*t-.5,y*t-.5,t+1,t+1);
  const isW=(x,y)=>y>=0&&y<ROWS&&x>=0&&x<COLS&&MAP[y][x]==='#';
  const seg=[];
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++){
    if(!isW(x,y)) continue;
    if(!isW(x,y-1))  seg.push([x*t,y*t,x*t+t,y*t]);
    if(!isW(x,y+1))  seg.push([x*t,(y+1)*t,x*t+t,(y+1)*t]);
    if(!isW(x-1,y))  seg.push([x*t,y*t,x*t,(y+1)*t]);
    if(!isW(x+1,y))  seg.push([(x+1)*t,y*t,(x+1)*t,(y+1)*t]);
  }
  const path=()=>{ g.beginPath(); for(const s of seg){ g.moveTo(s[0],s[1]); g.lineTo(s[2],s[3]); } };
  g.lineCap='round'; g.lineJoin='round';
  if(flash){
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,240,170,.3)'; g.lineWidth=t*.42; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(255,235,160,.85)'; g.lineWidth=t*.2; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='#fffbe6'; g.lineWidth=t*.09; g.stroke();
  } else {
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(40,255,140,.16)'; g.lineWidth=t*.44; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='rgba(80,255,170,.5)'; g.lineWidth=t*.2; g.stroke();
    path(); g.strokeStyle='#c9ffe3'; g.lineWidth=t*.09; g.stroke();
  }
  return c;
}

/* ============================== drawing ============================== */
const cv=$('cv'),ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
function circle(x,y,r){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,Math.max(.1,r),0,TAU); ctx.fill(); }
function rrect(x,y,w,h,r){
  ctx.beginPath();
  if(ctx.roundRect){ ctx.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r); return; }
  ctx.moveTo(x+r,y); ctx.lineTo(x+w-r,y); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+r,r);
  ctx.lineTo(x+w,y+h-r); ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x+w-r,y+h,r);
  ctx.lineTo(x+r,y+h); ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y+h-r,r);
  ctx.lineTo(x,y+r); ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+r,y,r); ctx.closePath();
}
function drawBackground(){
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#060a12'); g.addColorStop(.55,'#08130d'); g.addColorStop(1,'#0b1c11');
  ctx.fillStyle=g; ctx.fillRect(-30,-30,W+60,H+60);
  // moon
  const mx=W*.82,my=H*.17,mr=Math.min(W,H)*.07+16;
  let rg=ctx.createRadialGradient(mx,my,mr*.4,mx,my,mr*3);
  rg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(200,225,255,.18)'); rg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(200,225,255,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle=rg; circle(mx,my,mr*3);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(226,240,250,.9)'; circle(mx,my,mr);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(160,185,205,.35)';
  circle(mx-mr*.3,my-mr*.15,mr*.16); circle(mx+mr*.25,my+mr*.3,mr*.11); circle(mx+mr*.1,my-mr*.42,mr*.08);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(7,15,11,.5)';
  const cx1=(T*12)%(W+mr*4)-mr*2;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx1,my-mr*.25,mr*1.5,mr*.4,0,0,TAU); ctx.fill();
  const cx2=W-((T*8)%(W+mr*4))+mr*2;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx2,my+mr*.35,mr*1.2,mr*.32,0,0,TAU); ctx.fill();
  // fog
  for(const f of fog){
    const fg=ctx.createRadialGradient(f.x,f.y,f.r*.1,f.x,f.y,f.r);
    fg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(150,200,170,'+f.a+')'); fg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(150,200,170,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=fg; circle(f.x,f.y,f.r);
  }
  // spores
  for(const s of spores){
    const a=.1+.3*Math.abs(Math.sin(T*s.tw+s.ph));
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(168,255,200,'+a.toFixed(3)+')';
    circle(s.x+Math.sin(T*s.tw+s.ph)*7,s.y,s.s);
  }
}
function drawPit(){
  const t=tile,x0=9*t,y0=TROW*t;
  ctx.fillStyle='#040208'; rrect(x0+1.5,y0+2,3*t-3,t-4,6); ctx.fill();
  const cg=ctx.createRadialGradient(10.5*t,11.5*t,2,10.5*t,11.5*t,t*1.5);
  cg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,60,200,.4)'); cg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(120,60,200,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle=cg; circle(10.5*t,11.5*t,t*1.5);
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(150,90,230,'+(.25+.18*Math.sin(T*2.4)).toFixed(3)+')';
  ctx.lineWidth=2;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(10.5*t,11.5*t,t*(1.05+.06*Math.sin(T*2.4)),t*.42,0,0,TAU); ctx.stroke();
  drawSkull(10.5*t,11.5*t+Math.sin(T*2.4)*1.5,t*.36);
}
function drawSkull(x,y,s){
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x,y);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(180,140,255,.12)'; circle(0,0,s*1.5);
  ctx.fillStyle='#e9e6d4';
  circle(0,-s*.15,s*.6);
  rrect(-s*.36,s*.14,s*.72,s*.38,s*.12); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='#120a1e';
  circle(-s*.25,-s*.2,s*.17); circle(s*.25,-s*.2,s*.17);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,-s*.02); ctx.lineTo(-s*.1,s*.13); ctx.lineTo(s*.1,s*.13); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,10,30,.85)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,s*.06);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-s*.16,s*.2); ctx.lineTo(-s*.16,s*.48); ctx.moveTo(s*.16,s*.2); ctx.lineTo(s*.16,s*.48); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawDots(){
  const t=tile;
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++){
    const d=dots[y][x]; if(!d) continue;
    const cx=(x+.5)*t,cy=(y+.5)*t;
    if(d===1){
      const r=t*.085*(1+.22*Math.sin(T*5+x*1.7+y*2.3));
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(140,255,190,.2)'; circle(cx,cy,r*2.1);
      ctx.fillStyle='#a6ffcf'; circle(cx,cy,r);
    } else {
      const ph=T*6+x,y*.7;
      const r=t*.27*(1+.14*Math.sin(ph));
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(120,255,255,.16)'; circle(cx,cy,r*2.3);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(125,252,255,.8)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,t*.05);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx,cy,r*1.4,0,TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='#eaffff'; circle(cx,cy,r*.9);
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){ const a=ph+k*TAU/4;
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.85)'; circle(cx+Math.cos(a)*r*1.4,cy+Math.sin(a)*r*1.4,t*.035); }
    }
  }
}
function drawSoul(z,px,py,r){
  const bob=Math.sin(T*12+z.idx)*r*.1;
  ctx.save();
  for(let k=3;k>=1;k--){
    ctx.globalAlpha=.04*k; ctx.fillStyle=z.color;
    circle(px-Math.cos(z.dirA)*k*r*.6,py+bob-Math.sin(z.dirA)*k*r*.6,r*(.5-.09*k));
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  for(const sg of [-1,1]){
    const ex=px+sg*r*.32,ey=py+bob-r*.08;
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(200,255,255,.25)'; circle(ex,ey,r*.42);
    ctx.fillStyle='#eaffff'; circle(ex,ey,r*.2);
    ctx.fillStyle='#10202a'; circle(ex,ey,r*.09);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawZombie(z){
  const s=tile;
  let off=0,alpha=1;
  if(z.state==='rising'){ const pr=clamp(z.stateT/RISEDUR,0,1); off=(1-pr)*s*1.05; alpha=.35+.65*pr; }
  const px=z.x*s,py=z.y*s+off,r=s*.42;
  if(z.dx||z.dy) z.dirA=Math.atan2(z.dy,z.dx);
  const a=z.dirA;
  if(z.state==='soul'){ drawSoul(z,px,py,r); return; }
  const fright=frightT>0&&state!=='menu';
  let flash=false;
  if(fright&&frightT<1.6) flash=Math.sin(T*24)>0;
  const col=(fright&&!flash)?'#dfe9ee':z.color;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
  if(off<2){ ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.3)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(px,py+r*.85,r*.75,r*.26,0,0,TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  let tx=0,ty2=0;
  if(fright&&!flash){ tx=rnd(-1.2,1.2); ty2=rnd(-1.2,1.2); }
  ctx.translate(px+tx,py+ty2);
  // outstretched arms
  ctx.save(); ctx.rotate(a);
  ctx.strokeStyle=col; ctx.lineWidth=r*.34; ctx.lineCap='round';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(r*.2,-r*.45); ctx.lineTo(r*1,-r*.6);
  ctx.moveTo(r*.2,r*.45); ctx.lineTo(r*1,r*.6); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
  // body: dome + torn hem
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(0,-r*.08,r,Math.PI,0);
  ctx.lineTo(r,r*.5);
  for(let i=1;i<=4;i++){ const bx=r-(2*r*i)/4; const by=r*.5+(i%2?r*.34:r*.12)*z.jag[i-1]; ctx.lineTo(bx,by); }
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle=col; ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,r*.08); ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(5,10,8,.5)'; ctx.stroke();
  // eyes
  const exo=Math.cos(a)*r*.12,eyo=Math.sin(a)*r*.12;
  if(fright&&!flash){
    ctx.fillStyle='#1c2f3a';
    for(const sg of [-1,1]){ ctx.save(); ctx.translate(exo+sg*r*.34,-r*.18+eyo); ctx.fillRect(-r*.05,-r*.16,r*.1,r*.28); ctx.restore(); }
  } else {
    for(const sg of [-1,1]){
      const ex=exo+sg*r*.34,ey=-r*.18+eyo;
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; circle(ex,ey,r*.17);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(10,12,16,.9)'; circle(ex+Math.cos(a)*r*.07,ey+Math.sin(a)*r*.07,r*.09);
    }
  }
  // stitched mouth
  ctx.strokeStyle=(fright&&!flash)?'#243a46':'rgba(12,14,18,.75)'; ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,r*.07);
  const my=r*.3;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-r*.34,my); ctx.lineTo(r*.34,my);
  for(const tk of [-.18,0,.18]){ ctx.moveTo(tk*r,my-r*.1); ctx.lineTo(tk*r,my+r*.1); }
  ctx.stroke();
  // rot spots
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(8,12,10,.3)';
  for(const [sx,sy] of z.spot) circle(sx*r,sy*r-r*.08,r*.13);
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawPlayerBody(r,m){
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,217,60,.14)'; circle(0,0,r*1.5);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,0); ctx.arc(0,0,r,m,m-TAU); ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle='#ffd93b'; ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(140,100,0,.45)'; ctx.stroke();
}
function drawPlayer(){
  const t=tile,px=player.x*t,py=player.y*t,r=t*.42;
  if(state==='dying'){
    const p=clamp((deathT-.35)/1.1,0,1); if(p>=1) return;
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(px,py); ctx.rotate(p*5); const sc=1-p*.95; ctx.scale(sc,sc);
    drawPlayerBody(r,Math.min(Math.PI*.98,.25+p*Math.PI*.73));
    ctx.restore(); return;
  }
  if(player.dx||player.dy) player.lastA=Math.atan2(player.dy,player.dx);
  const a=player.lastA;
  const moving=(player.dx||player.dy)&&frostT<=0&&state==='playing';
  const m=moving?(.05+.33*Math.abs(Math.sin(T*9)))*Math.PI:.12;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(px,py); ctx.rotate(a);
  drawPlayerBody(r,m);
  // eyes (perpendicular to facing)
  for(const sg of [-1,1]){
    const ex=r*.12,ey=sg*r*.36;
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; circle(ex,ey,r*.13);
    ctx.fillStyle='#20242c'; circle(ex+r*.05,ey,r*.06);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawParts(){
  const t=tile;
  for(const p of parts){
    const a=clamp(p.life/p.max,0,1),px=p.x*t,py=p.y*t;
    if(p.type==='mist'){ ctx.fillStyle='rgba(150,95,225,'+(a*.3).toFixed(3)+')'; circle(px,py,p.size*(2-a)); }
    else if(p.type==='spark'){ ctx.globalAlpha=a; ctx.fillStyle=p.color; circle(px,py,p.size); ctx.globalAlpha=1; }
    else { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=a; ctx.translate(px,py); ctx.rotate(p.rot); ctx.fillStyle=p.color;
      ctx.fillRect(-p.size/2,-p.size/2,p.size,p.size); ctx.restore(); }
  }
}
function drawTexts(){
  ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
  for(const t of texts){
    const k=t.t/t.life,a=k<.7?1:(1-(k-.7)/.3);
    const px=clamp(t.x,1.6,COLS-2.6)*tile,py=t.y*tile-k*14;
    ctx.globalAlpha=a; ctx.font='bold '+t.size+"px 'Courier New',monospace";
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.6)'; ctx.fillText(t.txt,px+1.5,py+1.5);
    ctx.fillStyle=t.color; ctx.fillText(t.txt,px,py);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
}

/* ============================== update ============================== */
function updatePlaying(dt){
  frostT=Math.max(0,frostT-dt);
  if(frostT>0) return; // frozen breath before the dead move
  if(player.wantX===-player.dx&&player.wantY===-player.dy&&(player.dx||player.dy)){
    const tx=Math.floor(player.x),ty=Math.floor(player.y);
    if(isOpen(tx+player.wantX,ty+player.wantY)){ player.dx=player.wantX; player.dy=player.wantY; }
  }
  stepEntity(player,dt,PSPEED,playerDecide);
  eatDots();
  if(state!=='playing') return;
  if(frightT>0){ frightT=Math.max(0,frightT-dt); sirenAcc+=dt; while(sirenAcc>.4){ sirenAcc-=.4; sfxSiren(sirenN++); } }
  modeT-=dt;
  if(modeT<=0){ mode=mode==='chase'?'scatter':'chase'; modeT=mode==='chase'?Math.max(7,13-night):4; }
  for(const z of zombies) updateZombie(z,dt);
  checkCollide();
}
function heartbeat(){
  if(state!=='playing'||frightT>0||frostT>0) return;
  let md=1e9;
  for(const z of zombies) if(z.state==='normal') md=Math.min(md,Math.hypot(z.x-player.x,z.y-player.y));
  if(md<2.8&&T>nextBeat){ nextBeat=T+clamp(md*.16,.35,.9); tone(58,40,.13,'sine',.5); tone(52,34,.1,'sine',.4,.15); }
}
function update(dt){
  T+=dt;
  initAmbient(); updateAmbient(dt);
  if(shakeT>0) shakeT=Math.max(0,shakeT-dt);
  switch(state){
    case 'menu': looseZombies(dt); pitMist(dt); break;
    case 'playing': updatePlaying(dt); updateParts(dt); pitMist(dt); heartbeat(); break;
    case 'dying': deathT+=dt; updateParts(dt);
      if(deathT>1.9){ if(lives<=0) gameOver(); else { resetRound(); state='playing'; } }
      break;
    case 'clearing': clearT+=dt; looseZombies(dt); updateParts(dt);
      if(clearT>1.7){ night++; loadDots(); resetRound(); state='playing';
        pushText('NIGHT '+night,10.5,8.5,'#ffd93b',Math.round(tile*.9),2); syncHUD(true); }
      break;
    case 'gameover': looseZombies(dt); updateParts(dt); pitMist(dt); break;
    case 'paused': break;
  }
}

/* ============================== render ============================== */
function shake(dur,mag){ shakeT=dur; shakeDur=dur; shakeMag=mag; }
function render(){
  if(!W||!H) return;
  let shx=0,shy=0;
  if(shakeT>0){ const k=shakeMag*(shakeT/shakeDur); shx=rnd(-k,k); shy=rnd(-k,k); }
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(shx,shy);
  drawBackground();
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(ox,oy);
  const useFlash=state==='clearing'&&(Math.floor(clearT/.25)%2===1);
  ctx.drawImage(useFlash?mazeF:mazeN,-8,-8,COLS*tile+16,ROWS*tile+16);
  drawPit();
  drawDots();
  for(const z of zombies) drawZombie(z);
  if(state!=='menu'&&state!=='gameover') drawPlayer();
  drawParts();
  drawTexts();
  ctx.restore();
  // overlays tinted to maze area
  if(frostT>0&&state==='playing'){
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(170,220,255,'+((frostT/1.4)*.26).toFixed(3)+')';
    ctx.fillRect(ox-8,oy-8,COLS*tile+16,ROWS*tile+16);
  }
  if(frightT>0&&state==='playing'){
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(80,255,230,'+(.045+.04*Math.sin(T*7)).toFixed(3)+')';
    ctx.fillRect(ox-8,oy-8,COLS*tile+16,ROWS*tile+16);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ============================== HUD ============================== */
let lastScore='',lastHigh='',lastNight=-1,lastLives=-1;
function drawLives(){
  const n=Math.min(lives,5); let h='';
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++) h+='<span class="pac"></span>';
  if(lives>5) h+='<em>×'+lives+'</em>';
  $('livesBox').innerHTML=h;
}
function syncHUD(force){
  const s=String(score).padStart(6,'0');
  if(force||s!==lastScore){ lastScore=s; $('scoreV').textContent=s; }
  const h2=String(high).padStart(6,'0');
  if(force||h2!==lastHigh){ lastHigh=h2; $('highV').textContent=h2; $('startHigh').textContent=h2; }
  if(force||night!==lastNight){ lastNight=night; $('nightV').textContent=String(night); }
  if(force||lives!==lastLives){ lastLives=lives; drawLives(); }
}

/* ============================== input ============================== */
const KEYMAP={ArrowUp:[0,-1],KeyW:[0,-1],ArrowDown:[0,1],KeyS:[0,1],ArrowLeft:[-1,0],KeyA:[-1,0],ArrowRight:[1,0],KeyD:[1,0]};
function setDir(d){
  if(state==='menu'){ startGame(); }
  player.wantX=d[0]; player.wantY=d[1];
}
function primaryAction(){
  if(state==='menu'||state==='gameover') startGame();
  else if(state==='paused'){ state='playing'; $('pauseOv').classList.add('hidden'); tone(660,880,.1,'square',.15); }
}
function togglePause(){
  if(state==='playing'){ state='paused'; $('pauseOv').classList.remove('hidden'); }
  else if(state==='paused'){ state='playing'; $('pauseOv').classList.add('hidden'); }
}
window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  initAudio();
  const k=e.code;
  if(KEYMAP[k]){ e.preventDefault(); setDir(KEYMAP[k]); return; }
  if(e.repeat) return;
  if(k==='Enter'||k==='Space'){ e.preventDefault(); primaryAction(); }
  else if(k==='KeyP') togglePause();
  else if(k==='KeyM') toggleMute();
  else if(k==='KeyR') startGame();
});
let pStart=null;
$('wrap').addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{ initAudio(); pStart=[e.clientX,e.clientY]; });
window.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
  if(!pStart) return;
  const dx=e.clientX-pStart[0],dy=e.clientY-pStart[1]; pStart=null;
  if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>24){
    setDir(Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)?(dx>0?[1,0]:[-1,0]):(dy>0?[0,1]:[0,-1]));
  } else primaryAction();
});
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{ if(document.hidden&&state==='playing') togglePause(); });
window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{ if(state==='playing') togglePause(); });

/* ============================== boot ============================== */
function resize(){
  const r=$('wrap').getBoundingClientRect();
  W=Math.max(50,r.width); H=Math.max(50,r.height);
  DPR=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  cv.width=Math.round(W*DPR); cv.height=Math.round(H*DPR);
  ctx.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);
  tile=Math.max(5,Math.floor(Math.min((W-14)/COLS,(H-14)/ROWS)));
  ox=Math.floor((W-COLS*tile)/2); oy=Math.floor((H-ROWS*tile)/2);
  mazeN=buildMazeLayer(false); mazeF=buildMazeLayer(true);
}
window.addEventListener('resize',resize);

makeZombies();
[[13.5,12.5],[7.5,12.5],[3.5,5.5],[17.5,19.5]].forEach((p,i)=>{ zombies[i].x=p[0]; zombies[i].y=p[1]; });
loadDots();
resize();
$('sndLbl').textContent='M · SOUND '+(muted?'OFF':'ON');
syncHUD(true);

let last=performance.now();
function frame(now){
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  let dt=(now-last)/1000; last=now;
  if(dt>.05) dt=.05;
  update(dt); render();
}
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
